, I must first construct sequences
\{\zeta_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
and
\{\mathcal{H}_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
such that
\mathcal{H}_{n}\subseteq\mathcal{H}
,
\zeta_{n}\rightarrow 0
as
n\rightarrow\infty
,
n\zeta_{n}^{2}\rightarrow\infty
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, and

\displaystyle\Pi\left((\beta,\eta)\in[-B,B]\times\mathcal{H}_{n}:\sqrt{|\beta-\beta_{0}|^{2}+||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}^{2}}<D\zeta_{n}\middle|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n}\right)\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}1

(22)

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where
D>0
is a large constant. In relation to
Castillo 2012a
, this corresponds to verifying the first part of condition (C’). To establish this result, I will first show that it holds conditional on the sequence
\{(X_{i},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
and then apply the Bounded Convergence Theorem to obtain (
22
). To this end, let
p_{0,Y|XW}
be the true density of
Y
given
X
and
W
, let
P_{0,Y|XW}^{(n)}=\bigotimes_{i=1}^{n}P_{0,Y|x_{i},w_{i}}
with
P_{0,Y|x_{i},w_{i}}
denoting the probability distribution associated with conditional density
p_{0,Y|X,W}(\cdot|x_{i},w_{i})
, and let
P_{0,WX}^{\infty}
be the joint law of the i.i.d sequence
\{(X_{i},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
. I show that there exists a sequence
\{\zeta_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
such that
\zeta_{n}\rightarrow 0
as
n\rightarrow\infty
,
n\zeta_{n}^{2}\rightarrow\infty
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, and the following holds

\displaystyle\Pi\left(\sqrt{|\beta-\beta_{0}|^{2}+||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}^{2}}\geq D\zeta_{n}\middle|\{(Y_{i},x_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n}\right)\overset{P_{0,Y|XW}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(23)

conditionally given
P_{0,XW}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{(x_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{(X_{i},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where
D>0
is a large constant. Since
\liminf_{n\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}^{2}>0
for
P_{0,XW}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{(x_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{(X_{i},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
by the strong law of large numbers, a sufficient condition for (
23
) is

\displaystyle\Pi\left(\sqrt{||f(\cdot,\beta,\eta)-f(\cdot,\beta_{0},\eta_{0})||_{n,2}^{2}}\geq\tilde{D}\zeta_{n}\middle|\{(Y_{i},x_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n}\right)\overset{P_{0,Y|XW}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(24)

conditionally given
P_{0,XW}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{(x_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{(X_{i},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where
\tilde{D}>0
is a constant,
f(x,w,\beta,\eta)=x\beta+\eta(w)
for each
(\beta,\eta)\in[-B,B]\times\mathcal{H}
, and, with some abuse of notation, the norm
||\cdot||_{n,2}
is the empirical
L^{2}
norm over the design points
\{(x_{1},w_{1}),...,(x_{n},w_{n})\}
. Display (
24
) is asking for a posterior contraction rate in a fixed design regression model with independent Gaussian errors. An application Theorem 4 of
Ghosal and van der Vaart 2007
verifies that
\zeta_{n}=n^{-\min\{\alpha_{\eta},\alpha_{0,\eta}\}/(2\alpha_{\eta}+d_{w})}
satisfies (
24
). Indeed, Lemmas 3 and 4 of
van der Vaart and van Zanten 2011
imply that the supremum norm concentration function
\varphi_{\eta_{0}}
of the Matérn Gaussian process at
\eta_{0}
satisfies
\varphi_{\eta_{0}}(\zeta_{n})\leq n\zeta_{n}^{2}
, and, as a result, Theorem 4 of
Ghosal and van der Vaart 2007
can be verified for
d_{n}=||\cdot||_{n,2}
,
\varepsilon_{n}=\zeta_{n}
, and
\Theta_{n}=[-B,B]\times\mathcal{H}_{n}^{*}
with
\{\mathcal{H}_{n}^{*}\}_{n\geq 1}
being the sieves from Theorem 2.1 of
van der Vaart and van Zanten 2008a
(i.e.,
\mathcal{H}_{n}^{*}=\zeta_{n}C_{1}([0,1])+M_{n}\mathbb{H}_{\eta,1}
, where
C_{1}([0,1])=\{f\in C([0,1]):||f||_{\infty}\leq 1\}
is the unit ball in space of continuous functions
C([0,1])
equipped with the supremum norm
||\cdot||_{\infty}
,
\mathbb{H}_{\eta,1}
is the unit ball in the RKHS
(\mathbb{H}_{\eta},||\cdot||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}})
associated with the Gaussian process
\eta
, and
M_{n}
is a positive constant). This completes the construction of the candidate sequence
\{\zeta_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
. I now propose a sequence of sets
\{\mathcal{H}_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
such that

\displaystyle\Pi(\eta\in\mathcal{H}_{n}^{c}|\{(Y_{i},x_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n})\overset{P_{0,Y|XW}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(25)

for
P_{0,XW}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{(x_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{(X_{i},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. For each
n\geq 1
, let
\mathcal{H}_{n}=\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{n}\cap\left\{\eta\in\mathcal{H}:|G(m_{n,2},\eta)|\leq 2\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}\right\}
, where
\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{n}=M\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}\mathbb{H}_{\eta,1}+\gamma_{n}C_{1}^{a_{\eta}}([0,1]^{d_{w}})
with
C_{1}^{a_{\eta}}([0,1]^{d_{w}})
being the unit ball in the Hölder space
(C^{a_{\eta}}([0,1]^{d_{w}}),||\cdot||_{a_{\eta}})
for
a_{\eta}\in(d_{w}/2,\alpha_{\eta})
,
\mathbb{H}_{\eta,1}
being the unit ball in the RKHS
(\mathbb{H}_{\eta},||\cdot||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}})
,
\gamma_{n}=n^{-(\alpha_{\eta}-a_{\eta})/(2\alpha_{\eta}+d_{w})}
,
M>0
being a sufficiently large positive constant,
G(m_{n,2},\eta)
being a centered Gaussian random variable with variance
||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}^{2}
under
\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}
, and
\{m_{n,2}\}_{n\geq 1}
is a sequence in
(\mathbb{H}_{\eta},||\cdot||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}})
such that
||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}\leq 2\sqrt{n}\rho_{n}
and
||m_{n,2}-m_{02}||_{\infty}\leq\rho_{n}
for
\rho_{n}=n^{-\min\{\alpha_{\eta},\alpha_{0,2}\}/(2\alpha_{\eta}+d_{w})}
. The sequences
\{m_{n,2}\}_{n\geq 1}
and
\{\rho_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
exist because
\inf_{h\in\mathbb{H}_{\eta}:||h-m_{02}||_{\infty}<\rho_{n}}\frac{1}{2}||h||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}^{2}\leq n\rho_{n}^{2}
by Lemma 3 of
van der Vaart and van Zanten 2011
. Applying the same argument as Step 2 of Proposition
4
, I can show that
\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}(\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{n}^{c})\lesssim\exp(-Mn\zeta_{n}^{2})
. Moreover, the Gaussian tail bound
P(Z>z)\leq\exp(-z^{2}/2)
for
Z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
implies
\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}(|G(m_{n,2},\eta)|>2\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}})\leq\exp(-n\zeta_{n}^{2})
. Consequently, (
25
) holds by an application of Lemma 1 in
Ghosal and van der Vaart 2007
. Lemma 1 of
Ghosal and van der Vaart 2007
is valid because Kullback-Leibler balls of the form (3.5) in their paper are contained in empirical
L^{2}
balls
\{(\beta,\eta):||f(\cdot,\beta,\eta)-f(\cdot,\beta_{0},\eta_{0})||_{n,2}<\zeta_{n}\}
and
\Pi(||f(\cdot,\beta,\eta)-f(\cdot,\beta_{0},\eta_{0})||_{n,2}<\zeta_{n})\geq\exp(-n\zeta_{n}^{2})
holds when
\Pi_{\mathcal{B}}=U([-B,B])
,
\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}
is the law of a centered Matérn gaussian process, and
\varphi_{\eta_{0}}(\zeta_{n})\leq n\zeta_{n}^{2}
. Applying the law of total probability, (
23
) and (
25
) combine to establish

\displaystyle\Pi\left((\beta,\eta)\in[-B,B]\times\mathcal{H}_{n}:\sqrt{|\beta-\beta_{0}|^{2}+||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}^{2}}<D\zeta_{n}\middle|\{(Y_{i},x_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n}\right)\overset{P_{0,Y|XW}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}1

for
P_{0,XW}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{(x_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{(X_{i},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. This implies (
22
) holds by an application of the Bounded Convergence Theorem.

Step 2.
I verify (12.13) of
Ghosal and Van der Vaart 2017
, a result that synthesizes the local shape condition (N’) and the RKHS approximation condition (E) in
Castillo 2012a
. In my setting, this requires showing that

\displaystyle\sup_{(\beta,\eta)\in\mathcal{B}\times\mathcal{H}_{n}:\max\{|\beta-\beta_{0}|,||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}\}<D\zeta_{n}}\frac{|\bar{R}_{n}(\beta,\eta)|}{1+n|\beta-\beta_{0}|^{2}}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where

\displaystyle\bar{R}_{n}(\beta,\eta)=\bar{\ell}_{n}(\beta,\eta)-\bar{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\tilde{\eta}_{n}(\beta,\eta))-\sqrt{n}(\beta-\beta_{0})\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},m_{0})+\frac{1}{2}n(\beta-\beta_{0})^{2}\tilde{I}_{n}(m_{0})

with

\displaystyle\bar{\ell}_{n}(\beta,\eta)=-\frac{n}{2}\log 2\pi-\frac{n}{2}\log\sigma_{01}^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sigma_{01}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(Y_{i}-X_{i}\beta-\eta(w_{i}))^{2}

denoting the log-likelihood under the
(\beta,\eta)
-parametrization and

\displaystyle\tilde{\eta}_{n}(\beta,\eta)=\eta+(\beta-\beta_{0})m_{n,2}

for each
(\beta,\eta)\in[-B,B]\times\mathcal{H}
. Letting
t=\beta-\beta_{0}
, a second-order Taylor expansion of
\ell_{n}(\beta_{0}+t,\eta)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\eta-tm_{n,2})
around
t=0
reveals that

\displaystyle\bar{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0}+t,\eta)-\bar{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\eta+tm_{n,2})

\displaystyle=t\frac{1}{\sigma_{01}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(U_{i}-(\eta(w_{i})-\eta_{0}(w_{i})))(X_{i}-m_{n,2}(w_{i}))

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\frac{1}{\sigma_{01}^{2}}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{n}(X_{i}-m_{n2}(w_{i}))^{2}+2\sum_{i=1}^{n}(X_{i}-m_{n,2}(w_{i}))m_{n,2}(w_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}(\beta-\beta_{0})T_{n,1}-\frac{1}{2}n(\beta-\beta_{0})^{2}T_{n,2},

where

\displaystyle T_{n,1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{01}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(U_{i}-(\eta(w_{i})-\eta_{0}(w_{i})))(X_{i}-m_{n,2}(w_{i}))

and

\displaystyle T_{n,2}=\frac{1}{\sigma_{01}^{2}n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(X_{i}-m_{n2}(w_{i}))^{2}+\frac{2}{n\sigma_{01}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(X_{i}-m_{n,2}(w_{i}))m_{n,2}(w_{i}).

Consequently, to verify (12.13) of
Ghosal and Van der Vaart 2017
, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\sup_{(\beta,\eta)\in\mathcal{B}\times\mathcal{H}_{n}:\max\{|\beta-\beta_{0}|,||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}\}<D\zeta_{n}}\left\{\frac{\left|\sqrt{n}(\beta-\beta_{0})\right|}{1+n|\beta-\beta_{0}|^{2}}\left|T_{n,1}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},m_{0})\right|\right\}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(26)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{(\beta,\eta)\in\mathcal{B}\times\mathcal{H}_{n}:\max\{|\beta-\beta_{0}|,||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}\}<D\zeta_{n}}\left\{\frac{n\left|\beta-\beta_{0}\right|^{2}}{1+n|\beta-\beta_{0}|^{2}}\left|T_{n,2}-\tilde{I}_{n}(m_{0})\right|\right\}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(27)

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. I first show condition (
26
). Since
X_{i}-m_{n,2}(w_{i})=X_{i}-m_{02}(w_{i})+m_{02}(w_{i})-m_{n,2}(w_{i})
for
i=1,...,n
, I know that

\displaystyle\left|T_{n,1}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},m_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{\sigma_{01}^{2}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}(m_{02}(w_{i})-m_{n,2}(w_{i}))\right|+\left|\frac{1}{\sigma_{01}^{2}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\eta(w_{i})-\eta_{0}(w_{i}))\varepsilon_{i2}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\frac{1}{\sigma_{01}^{2}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\eta(w_{i})-\eta_{0}(w_{i}))(m_{n,2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))\right|

By Chebyshev’s inequality, the fact that
Var_{P_{0}}[U|X,W]=\sigma_{01}^{2}
, and
||m_{n,2}-m_{02}||_{\infty}\rightarrow 0
as
n\rightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}(m_{02}(w_{i})-m_{n,2}(w_{i}))\right|=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
. Since a symmetric argument to that encountered in Proposition
4
implies that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{D\zeta_{n}}\sqrt{\log N(\tau,\mathcal{H}_{n},||\cdot||_{n,2})}d\tau\rightarrow 0,

for
\zeta_{n}=n^{-\min\{\alpha_{\eta},\alpha_{0,\eta}\}/(2\alpha_{\eta}+d_{w})}
with
\alpha_{\eta}>d_{w}/2
and
\alpha_{0,\eta}>\alpha_{\eta}/2+d_{w}/4
, it follows by Corollary 2.2.9 of
van der Vaart and Wellner 2023
that

\displaystyle\sup_{\eta\in\mathcal{H}_{n}:||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}<D\zeta_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}(\eta(w_{i})-\eta_{0}(w_{i}))\right|=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Finally, an application of Cauchy-Schwarz and the fact that
||\cdot||_{n,2}\leq||\cdot||_{\infty}
reveals that

\displaystyle\sup_{\eta\in\mathcal{H}_{n}:||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}<D\zeta_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\eta(w_{i})-\eta_{0}(w_{i}))(m_{n,2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}\rho_{n}=o(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where convergence holds because
\alpha_{\eta}>d_{w}/2
and
\alpha_{0,\eta}>\alpha_{\eta}/2+d_{w}/4
implies
\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}=o(n^{1/4})
and
\alpha_{0,2}>\alpha_{\eta}/2+d_{w}/4
implies
\rho_{n}=o(n^{-1/4})
. This verifies (
26
). For (
27
),
X_{i}-m_{n,2}(w_{i})=X_{i}-m_{02}(w_{i})+m_{02}(w_{i})-m_{n,2}(w_{i})
implies that
T_{n,2}-\tilde{I}_{n}(m_{0})
satisfies

\displaystyle\left|T_{n,2}-\tilde{I}_{n}(m_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}(m_{n,2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))\right|+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{n,2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}m_{02}(w_{i})\right|+\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{n,2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))m_{02}(w_{i})\right|

Since
||m_{n,2}-m_{02}||_{\infty}\rightarrow 0
as
n\rightarrow\infty
and
\sup_{w\in[0,1]^{d_{w}}}Var_{P_{0}}(\varepsilon_{2}|W=w)<\infty
, an application of Chebyshev’s inequality reveals that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}(m_{n,2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))\right|\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0\quad\text{and}\quad\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}m_{02}(w_{i})\right|\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Moreover, the convergence
||m_{n,2}-m_{02}||_{\infty}\rightarrow 0
as
n\rightarrow\infty
implies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{n,2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{2}\longrightarrow 0\quad\text{and}\quad\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{n,2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))m_{02}(w_{i})\right|\longrightarrow 0

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. This verifies (
27
).

Step 3.
The next step is to verify (12.14) of
Ghosal and Van der Vaart 2017
. In my setting, this requires

\displaystyle\sup_{(\beta,\eta)\in\mathcal{B}\times\mathcal{H}_{n}:\max\{|\beta-\beta_{0}|,||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}\}<D\zeta_{n}}\frac{|\log(d\Pi_{n,\beta,\mathcal{H}}(\eta)/d\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}(\eta))|}{1+n|\beta-\beta_{0}|^{2}}\longrightarrow 0,

where
\Pi_{n,\beta,\mathcal{H}}
is the law of
\tilde{\eta}_{n}(\beta,\eta)
under
\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}
for
\beta
fixed. Since
\{m_{n,2}\}_{n\geq 1}
is a sequence in
(\mathbb{H}_{\eta},||\cdot||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}})
, I can apply the Cameron-Martin theorem to obtain

\displaystyle\log\frac{d\Pi_{n,\beta,\mathcal{H}}(\eta)}{d\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}(\eta)}=(\beta-\beta_{0})G(m_{n,2},\eta)-\frac{1}{2}|\beta-\beta_{0}|^{2}||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}^{2},

where
G(m_{n,2},\eta)\sim\mathcal{N}(0,||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}^{2})
for each
n\geq 1
under
\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}
. Since
\{\eta:|G(m_{n,2},\eta)|\leq 2\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}\}\subseteq\mathcal{H}_{n}
and
||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}\leq 2\sqrt{n}\rho_{n}
, I know that

\displaystyle\sup_{(\beta,\eta)\in\mathcal{B}\times\mathcal{H}_{n}:\max\{|\beta-\beta_{0}|,||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}\}<D\zeta_{n}}\frac{|\log(d\Pi_{n,\beta,\mathcal{H}}(\eta)/d\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}(\eta))|}{1+n|\beta-\beta_{0}|^{2}}\leq 2\zeta_{n}\rho_{n}+\frac{\rho_{n}^{2}}{n}=o(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
because
\rho_{n}\rightarrow 0
and
\zeta_{n}\rightarrow 0
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

Step 4.
The results from Steps 1–3 permit an application of Theorem 12.9 of
Ghosal and Van der Vaart 2017
provided that I can show that the posterior satisfies

\displaystyle\sup_{\beta\in[-B,B]:|\beta-\beta_{0}|<D\zeta_{n}}\Pi\left(\tilde{\eta}_{n}(\beta,\{\eta\in\mathcal{H}_{n}:||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}<D\zeta_{n}\})^{c}\middle|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n},\beta=\beta_{0}\right)\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. By definition of
\tilde{\eta}_{n}(\beta,\eta)
, I know that

\displaystyle\tilde{\eta}_{n}(\beta,\{\eta\in\mathcal{H}_{n}:||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}<D\zeta_{n}\})

\displaystyle=\{\eta\in\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{n}(\beta):||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}<D\zeta_{n}\}

\displaystyle\quad\cap\{\eta:|G(m_{n,2},\eta-(\beta-\beta_{0})m_{n,2})|\leq 2\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}\},

where
\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{n}(\beta)=\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{n}+(\beta-\beta_{0})m_{n,2}
. Hence, the union bound implies that it suffices to show

\displaystyle\sup_{|\beta-\beta_{0}|<D\zeta_{n}}\Pi\left(\{\eta\in\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{n}(\beta):||\eta-\eta_{0}||_{n,2}<D\zeta_{n}\}^{c}\middle|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n},\beta=\beta_{0}\right)\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(28)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{|\beta-\beta_{0}|<D\zeta_{n}}\Pi\left(\{|G(m_{n,2},\eta-(\beta-\beta_{0})m_{n,2})|\leq 2\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}\}^{c}\middle|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n},\beta=\beta_{0}\right)\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(29)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. For (
28
) (which corresponds to the second part of (C’) in
Castillo 2012a
), the sets
\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{n}(\beta)
, where
|\beta-\beta_{0}|<D\zeta_{n}
, are, up to constants, the same as
\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{n}
because
\sup_{|\beta-\beta_{0}|<D\zeta_{n}}||(\beta-\beta_{0})m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}\leq 2D\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}\rho_{n}=o(1)
. Consequently, a similar argument to Step 1 can be used to check (
28
). For (
29
), I apply Lemma 13 of
Ray and van der Vaart 2020
to conclude that
G(m_{n,2},\eta-(\beta-\beta_{0})m_{n,2})\sim\mathcal{N}((\beta-\beta_{0})||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}^{2},||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}^{2})
for
\eta\sim\Pi_{\mathcal{H}}
and
\beta
fixed, and, as a result,
G(m_{n,2},\eta-(\beta-\beta_{0})m_{n,2})=G(m_{n,2},\eta)+(\beta-\beta_{0})||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}^{2}
for
\beta
fixed. Since the inequality
\sup_{|\beta-\beta_{0}|<D\zeta_{n}}|\beta-\beta_{0}|\cdot||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}\leq 2D\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}\rho_{n}\leq\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}
holds for large
n
(as
\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}\rho_{n}\rightarrow 0
as
n\rightarrow\infty
and
n\zeta_{n}^{2}\rightarrow\infty
as
n\rightarrow\infty
), it follows that, for
n
large,

\displaystyle\sup_{|\beta-\beta_{0}|<D\zeta_{n}}\Pi\left(\upsilon:|G(m_{n,2},\upsilon+(\beta-\beta_{0})m_{n,2})|>2\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}||m_{n,2}||_{\mathbb{H}_{\eta}}\middle|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n},\beta=\beta_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Pi\left(\upsilon:|G(m_{n,2},\upsilon)|>\sqrt{n}\zeta_{n}||m_{n,2}||_{n,2}\middle|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n},\beta=\beta_{0}\right)\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where the convergence in probability holds by a similar argument to Step 1. This verifies all the conditions for Theorem 12.9 in
Ghosal and Van der Vaart 2017
, and, as a result, the proof is complete.
∎

Appendix C
Proof of Theorem
2

Proof of Theorem
2
.

The proof has two steps.
Step 1
proves Part 1 by relating the objective function to least squares.
Step 2
uses the results from Step 1 to prove the second statement of the theorem.

Step 1.
Let
Z=(Y,X)^{\prime}
. I use the definition of Kullback Leibler divergence and the expression for
p_{\beta,m}
to conclude that

\displaystyle E_{P_{0}}\left[KL\left(p_{0,YX|W}(\cdot|W),p_{\beta,m}(\cdot|W)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=E_{P_{0}}[\log p_{0,YX|W}(Y,X|W)]-E_{P_{0}}[\log p_{\beta,m}(Y,X|W)]

\displaystyle=E_{P_{0}}[\log p_{0,YX|W}(Y,X|W)]+\frac{1}{2}\log 4\pi^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\log\det V(\beta)+\frac{1}{2}E_{P_{0}}[(Z-m(W))^{\prime}V^{-1}(\beta)(Z-m(W))].

Note that the expectation is finite for all
(\beta,m)\in\mathcal{B}\times\mathcal{M}
by Assumption
1
,
\mathcal{M}\subseteq L^{2}(\mathcal{W})\times L^{2}(\mathcal{W})
, and the fact that
\det V(\beta)=\sigma_{01}^{2}\sigma_{02}^{2}>0
. Next, I write
Z=m_{0}(W)+\varepsilon
and note that
\varepsilon=Z-m_{0}(W)
satisfies
E_{P_{0}}[\varepsilon|W]=0
. Consequently, the law of iterated expectations implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}E_{P_{0}}\left[(Z-m(W))^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}(Z-m(W))\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{2}E_{P_{0}}\left[(\varepsilon+m_{0}(W)-m(W))^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}(\varepsilon+m_{0}(W)-m(W))\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{2}E_{P_{0}}[(m(W)-m_{0}(W))^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}(m(W)-m_{0}(W))]+\frac{1}{2}E_{P_{0}}[\varepsilon^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}\varepsilon].

Assumption
1
and
V(\beta)^{-1}
being symmetric and positive-definite guarantees
E_{P_{0}}[\varepsilon^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}\varepsilon]\in(0,\infty)
. Positive-definiteness of
V(\beta)
follows from Sylvester’s criterion because
\sigma_{01}^{2}+\beta^{2}\sigma_{01}^{2}>0
and
\det V(\beta)=\sigma_{01}^{2}\sigma_{02}^{2}>0
. Hence, I can combine this with the above to conclude that there is a constant
C(\beta)\in(-\infty,\infty)
such that

\displaystyle E_{P_{0}}\left[KL\left(p_{0}(\cdot|W),p_{\beta,m}(\cdot|W)\right)\right]=C(\beta)+\frac{1}{2}E_{P_{0}}[(m(W)-m_{0}(W))^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}(m(W)-m_{0}(W))].

Consequently, it suffices to solve

\displaystyle\min_{m\in\mathcal{M}}E_{P_{0}}\left[(m(W)-m_{0}(W))^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}(m(W)-m_{0}(W))\right].

(30)

I establish that
m_{0}
uniquely solves (
30
). First,
m_{0}
minimizes the objective function because

\displaystyle E_{P_{0}}\left[(m(W)-m_{0}(W))^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}(m(W)-m_{0}(W))\right]\geq 0

for all
m\in\mathcal{M}
with equality at
m=m_{0}
and the nuisance parameter space is chosen so that
m_{0}\in\mathcal{M}
(i.e.,
m_{0}
is feasible). For uniqueness, I note that the positive-definiteness of
V(\beta)^{-1}
implies

\displaystyle E_{P_{0}}\left[(m(W)-m_{0}(W))^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}(m(W)-m_{0}(W))\right]

\displaystyle\quad\geq\lambda_{min}(V(\beta)^{-1})\left\{E_{P_{0}}[(m_{1}(W)-m_{01}(W))^{2}]+E_{P_{0}}[(m_{2}(W)-m_{02}(W))^{2}]\right\}

(31)

and

\displaystyle E_{P_{0}}\left[(m(W)-m_{0}(W))^{\prime}V(\beta)^{-1}(m(W)-m_{0}(W))\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\lambda_{max}(V(\beta)^{-1})\left\{E_{P_{0}}[(m_{1}(W)-m_{01}(W))^{2}]+E_{P_{0}}[(m_{2}(W)-m_{02}(W))^{2}]\right\},

(32)

Using the definition of the
L^{2}
-norm, it follows that

\displaystyle E_{P_{0}}[(m_{1}(W)-m_{01}(W))^{2}]+E_{P_{0}}[(m_{2}(W)-m_{02}(W))^{2}]>0

if and only if there exists
j\in\{1,2\}
such that

\displaystyle P_{0}(m_{j}(W)\neq m_{0j}(W))>0.

From this, I conclude that
m_{0}
is the unique solution to (
30
) in an almost-sure sense.

Step 2.
Based on the results of
Step 1
, it is sufficient to solve

\displaystyle\min_{\beta\in\mathcal{B}}E_{P_{0}}[KL(p_{0}(\cdot|W),p_{\beta,m_{0}}(\cdot|W))].

to obtain the second assertion of the theorem. The factorization of a bivariate normal distribution into its conditional and marginal distributions offers further simplification because it reduces the problem to solving

\displaystyle\min_{\beta\in\mathcal{B}}E_{P_{0}}\left[\frac{1}{2\sigma_{01}^{2}}\left(Y-m_{01}(W)-(X-m_{02}(W))\beta\right)^{2}\right].

Adding and subtracting
(X-m_{02}(W))\beta_{0}
and invoking Part 4 of Assumption
1
, I can find a constant
C\in(-\infty,\infty)
that does not depend on
\beta
such that

\displaystyle E_{P_{0}}\left[\frac{1}{2\sigma_{01}^{2}}\left(Y-m_{01}(W)-(X-m_{02}(W))\beta\right)^{2}\right]=C+\frac{1}{2\sigma_{01}^{2}}(\beta-\beta_{0})^{2}E_{P_{0}}[(X-m_{02}(W))^{2}],

and, as a result, it suffices to solve the least squares problem

\displaystyle\min_{\beta\in\mathcal{B}}\left\{\frac{1}{2\sigma_{01}^{2}}(\beta-\beta_{0})^{2}E_{P_{0}}[(X-m_{02}(W))^{2}]\right\}.

(33)

Since the objective function is nonnegative and equal to zero when
\beta=\beta_{0}
(feasible because
\beta_{0}\in\mathcal{B}
), it follows that
\beta_{0}
is a solution to (
33
). Uniqueness follows from Part 2 of Assumption
1
because the restriction
E_{P_{0}}[(X-m_{02}(W))^{2}]\in[\underline{c},\overline{c}]
implies that the objective function is equal to
0
if and only if
\beta=\beta_{0}
.
∎

Appendix D
Multivariate Extension

The main text assumes that
\sigma_{01}^{2}
is known and treats
X
as a scalar random variable, however, this was largely for expositional reasons and can be relaxed with minor modifications to the assumptions. This section presents an extension of Theorem
1
to allow for unknown
\sigma_{01}^{2}
and multivariate
X
.

D.1
DGP, Bayesian Model, and Assumptions about Posterior Concentration

D.1.1
Data-Generating Process

Assumption
5
extends Assumption
1
to allow for multivariate
X
and unknown
\sigma_{01}^{2}
. The existence of fourth moments for the projection errors
Y-E_{P_{0}}[Y|X]
(i.e., the first condition in Part 4) relates to the estimation of
\sigma_{01}^{2}
. For notation, the symbol
||\cdot||_{2}
refers to the Euclidean norm.

Assumption 5
.

The distribution of the data
P_{0}
satisfies the following restrictions:

1.

The distribution of
Y
given
X
and
W
satisfies
E_{P_{0}}[Y|X,W]=X^{\prime}\beta_{0}+\eta_{0}(W)
and
Var_{P_{0}}[Y|X,W]=\sigma_{01}^{2}
for some
(\beta_{0},\eta_{0},\sigma_{01}^{2})\in\mathcal{B}\times\mathcal{H}\times\mathbb{R}_{++}
, where
\mathcal{B}\subseteq\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}
,
\mathcal{H}\subseteq L^{2}(\mathcal{W})
.

2.

There exists constants
0<\underline{c}<\overline{c}<\infty
such that
\underline{c}<\lambda_{min}(E_{P_{0}}[(X-E_{P_{0}}[X|W])(X-E_{P_{0}}[X|W])^{\prime}])
and
\lambda_{max}(E_{P_{0}}[(X-E_{P_{0}}[X|W])(X-E_{P_{0}}[X|W])^{\prime}])\leq\overline{c}
.

3.

The conditional distribution of
(Y,X^{\prime})^{\prime}
given
W
has a density
p_{0,YX|W}
with respect to the Lebesgue measure and
E_{P_{0}}|\log p_{0,YX|W}(Y,X|W)|\in(0,\infty)

4.

The projection errors
Y-E_{P_{0}}[Y|W]
and
X-E_{P_{0}}[X|W]
are such that
E_{P_{0}}[(Y-E_{P_{0}}[Y|W])^{4}]\in(0,\infty)
and
E_{P_{0}}[||X-E_{P_{0}}[X|W]||_{2}^{4}]\in(0,\infty)
.

D.1.2
Bayesian Model

I extend the Bayesian model from Section
2
to accommodate multivariate
X
and unknown
\sigma_{01}^{2}
. I parametrize the sampling model in terms of precision
\xi_{0}=1/\sigma_{01}^{2}
, which means that the extension of the sampling model is

\displaystyle Y_{i}|\{X_{i},W_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n},\beta,m,\xi\overset{ind}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(m_{1}(W_{i})+(X_{i}-m_{2}(W_{i}))^{\prime}\beta,\xi^{-1})

(34)

\displaystyle X_{i}|\{W_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n},\beta,m,\xi\overset{ind}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(m_{2}(W_{i}),\Sigma_{02})

(35)

for
i=1,...,n
, where
\beta\in\mathcal{B}\subseteq\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}
,
m=(m_{1},m_{2})\in\mathcal{M}_{1}\times\prod_{k=1}^{d_{x}}\mathcal{M}_{2,k}\subseteq L^{2}(\mathcal{W})^{d_{x}+1}
,
\xi\in\Xi
with
\Xi=[\overline{\sigma}^{-2},\underline{\sigma}^{-2}]
for some
0<\underline{\sigma}<\overline{\sigma}<\infty
, and
\Sigma_{02}
is a known positive-definite matrix. The first display imposes the multivariate version of the
Robinson 1988
transformation. The second display is included solely for identifiability of
m_{02}
, and, as a result, it is without loss of generality to treat
\Sigma_{02}
as known (e.g.,
\Sigma_{02}=I_{d_{x}}
) as it has no intrinsic meaning (like
\sigma_{02}^{2}
in the main text). Let
L_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)
be the (quasi-)likelihood implied by (
34
)–(
35
) and let
\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)=\log L_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)
. For the prior, I assume that
(\beta,\xi)\sim\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}
,
m\sim\Pi_{\mathcal{M}}
, and
(\beta,\xi)\mathchoice{\mathrel{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\perp$\hss}\mkern 2.0mu{\displaystyle\perp}}}{\mathrel{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\perp$\hss}\mkern 2.0mu{\textstyle\perp}}}{\mathrel{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\perp$\hss}\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptstyle\perp}}}{\mathrel{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\perp$\hss}\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\perp}}}m
. Consequently, the joint prior is
\Pi=\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\otimes\Pi_{\mathcal{M}}
. The following extends Assumption
2
.

Assumption 6
.

The following conditions hold:

1.

\int_{\mathcal{B}\times\mathcal{M}}L_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)d\Pi(\beta,\xi,m)\in(0,\infty)
a.e.
[P_{0}^{\infty}]
.

2.

\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}
has a Lebesgue density
\pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}
that is continuous and positive over a neighborhood of
(\beta_{0},\xi_{0})
.

Assumption
6
implies that the posterior
\Pi((\beta,\xi,m)\in\cdot|\{(Y_{i},X_{i}^{\prime},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n})
is obtained via Bayes rule,

\displaystyle\Pi((\beta,\xi,m)\in A|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n})=\frac{\int_{A}L_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)d\Pi(\beta,\xi,m)}{\int_{\mathcal{B}\times\mathcal{M}}L_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)d\Pi(\beta,\xi,m)},

where
A
is a measurable set.

D.1.3
Assumptions on Nuisance Posterior

The next two assumptions extend Assumptions
3
and
4
to accommodate multivariate
X
and unknown
\sigma_{01}^{2}
. The latter explains why Assumption
8
is stated slightly differently to Assumption
4
because estimating the scale parameter
\sigma_{01}^{2}
introduces an additional multiplier empirical process that involves the interaction between
U
and
m_{1}-m_{01}
. However, the equicontinuity conditions stated in Assumption
8
nests Assumption
4
because
U=\varepsilon_{1}-\varepsilon_{2}^{\prime}\beta_{0}
. For notation,
B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},\delta)=\{m\in\mathcal{M}:\max\{||m_{1}-m_{01}||_{n,2},||m_{2,1}-m_{02,1}||_{n,2},...,||m_{2,d_{x}}-m_{02,d_{x}}||_{n,2}\}<\delta\}
with
m_{2,k}
,
k\in\{1,...,d_{x}\}
, being components of
m_{2}
, and
||\cdot||_{op}
is the operator norm of a matrix induced by the Euclidean norm
||\cdot||_{2}
(i.e., the spectral norm).

Assumption 7
.

There exists a sequence
\{\delta_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
such that

\displaystyle\delta_{n}=o(n^{-1/4})

and

\displaystyle\Pi(B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n})\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where
D>0
is a large constant.

Assumption 8
.

There are sets
\{\mathcal{M}_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
such that

\displaystyle\Pi(\mathcal{M}_{n}|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n})\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}1

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i1}(m_{1}(w_{i})-m_{01}(w_{i}))\right|\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}(m_{1}(w_{i})-m_{01}(w_{i}))\right|\right|_{2}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i1}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))\right|\right|_{2}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\right|\right|_{op}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

D.2
Extensions of Lemma
1
and
2
to Multivariate
X
and Unknown
\sigma_{01}^{2}

This section extends Lemmas
1
and
2
to allow for unknown
\sigma_{01}^{2}
and multivariate
X
. I start with some notation. Let

\displaystyle\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)=\begin{pmatrix}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta,\xi,m)\\
\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta,\xi,m),\end{pmatrix}

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta,\xi,m)=\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta^{\prime}}\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)=\xi\sum_{i=1}^{n}(Y_{i}-m_{1}(w_{i})-(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta)(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta,\xi,m)=\frac{\partial}{\partial\xi}\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)=\frac{n}{2\xi}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(Y_{i}-m_{1}(w_{i})-(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta)^{2}.

Let

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)=\begin{pmatrix}\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta,\xi,m)&\tilde{I}_{n,12}(\beta,\xi,m)\\
\tilde{I}_{n,21}(\beta,\xi,m)&\tilde{I}_{n,22}(\beta,\xi,m)\end{pmatrix},

be a
(d_{x}+1)\times(d_{x}+1)
symmetric matrix, where

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta,\xi,m)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial\beta\partial\beta^{\prime}}\frac{1}{n}\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)=\xi\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))^{\prime}

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{n,12}(\beta,\xi,m)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial\beta\partial\xi}\frac{1}{n}\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)=-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(Y_{i}-m_{1}(w_{i})-(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta)(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{n,22}(\beta,\xi,m)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial\xi^{2}}\frac{1}{n}\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)=\frac{1}{2\xi^{2}}.

The first result extends Lemma
1
to accommodate multivariate
X
and unknown
\sigma_{01}^{2}
.

Lemma 4
.

Let
\{\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
be the sets defined in Assumptions
7
and
8
. If Assumption
5
holds, then the following holds:

1.

The collection of functions
\{\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m):m\in\mathcal{M}\}
satisfies

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{2}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

2.

The collection of functions
\{\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m):m\in\mathcal{M}\}
satisfies

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\left|\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{op}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

3.

Let
\underline{c}_{0}=\underline{c}/\sigma_{01}^{2}
and
\overline{c}_{0}=\overline{c}/\sigma_{01}^{2}
, where
\overline{c}
and
\underline{c}
are the constants Part 2 of Assumption
5
. The following statements hold:

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\inf_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{\mathcal{M},\infty}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\lambda_{min}(\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m))\geq\underline{c}_{0}\right)\rightarrow 1

and

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{\mathcal{M},\infty}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\lambda_{max}(\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m))\leq\overline{c}_{0}\right)\rightarrow

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

4.

Let
\theta=(\beta^{\prime},\xi)^{\prime}
and let
R_{n}(\theta,m)
be given by

\displaystyle R_{n}(\theta,m)=\ell_{n}(\theta,m)-\ell_{n}(\theta_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})^{\prime}\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})+\frac{n}{2}(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})(\theta-\theta_{0}).

If
M_{n}\rightarrow\infty
arbitrarily slowly, then

\displaystyle\sup_{(\theta,m)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},M_{n}/\sqrt{n})\times(B_{\mathcal{M},\infty}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n})}\left|R_{n}(\theta,m)\right|\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

Proof.

The proof has four steps with each step corresponding to the appropriate part of the lemma.

Step 1.
Applying the triangle inequality, I know that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|

Consequently, it suffices to show that the following holds

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{2}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(36)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(37)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. For (
36
), I directly expand
\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})
and apply the triangle inequality to conclude

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{1}(w_{i})-m_{01}(w_{i}))\varepsilon_{i2}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))(U_{i}-\varepsilon_{i2}^{\prime}\beta_{0})\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{1}(w_{i})-m_{01}(w_{i}))(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}

Assumptions
7
and
8
then imply that

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{2}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
. For (
37
), let

\displaystyle U_{i}(m)=Y_{i}-m_{1}(w_{i})-(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta_{0}

and observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{n}}{2\xi_{0}}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left((m_{1}(w_{i})-m_{01}(w_{i}))+(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta_{0}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}[(m_{1}(w_{i})-m_{01}(w_{i}))+(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta_{0}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left((m_{1}(w_{i})-m_{01}(w_{i}))+(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta_{0}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}[(m_{1}(w_{i})-m_{01}(w_{i}))+(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta_{0}],

where I have used that
U_{i}(m_{0})=U_{i}
. Assumptions
7
and
8
then imply that

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\cap\mathcal{M}_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. This verifies (
37
).

Step 2.
Adding and subtracting
m_{02}(w_{i})
, the block
\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)
admits the decomposition:

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)

\displaystyle=\tilde{I}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})+\frac{\xi_{0}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\xi_{0}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}-\frac{\xi_{0}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))\varepsilon_{i2}^{\prime}

Consequently, the triangle inequality and the fact that
\lambda_{max}(A)=\lambda_{max}(A^{\prime})
reveals

\displaystyle\left|\left|\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{op}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\right|\right|_{op}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\right|\right|_{op}.

Since
\delta_{n}=o(1)
,

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\left|\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\right|\right|_{op}\longrightarrow 0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Moreover, Assumption
8
implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\left|\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i2}(m_{2}(w_{i})-m_{02}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\right|\right|_{op}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. This establishes Part 2.

Step 3.
I first show the result for the minimum eigenvalue. Since

\displaystyle\lambda_{min}(\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m))=||\tilde{I}_{n,11}^{-1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)||_{op},

it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\left|\left|\tilde{I}_{n,11}^{-1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\tilde{I}_{n,11}^{-1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{op}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

(38)

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
. Indeed, Parts 2 and 4 of Assumption
5
, Chebyshev’s inequality, and the strong law of large numbers implies that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\tilde{I}_{n,11}^{-1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{op}\geq\underline{c}_{0}

with
P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}
-probability approaching one, conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
. The convergence (
38
) holds by Part 2 of the lemma and the continuous mapping theorem. Next, I show the result for the maximum eigenvalue. Part 4 of Assumption
5
and Chebyshev’s inequality implies

\displaystyle||\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})||_{op}\leq\overline{c}_{0}

with
P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}
-probability approaching one for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Consequently, it follows by Part 2 of this lemma, the triangle inequality, and the fact that
||A||_{op}=\lambda_{max}(A)
that with
P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}
-probability approaching one,

\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\lambda_{max}\left(\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)\leq\overline{c}_{0}

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

Step 4.
Let
\theta=(\beta^{\prime},\xi)^{\prime}
. Applying a second-order Taylor expansion, I know that

\displaystyle\ell_{n}(\theta,m)-\ell_{n}(\theta_{0},m)=\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m)-\frac{1}{2}n(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m)(\theta-\theta_{0}),

where
\tilde{\theta}_{n}
is on the line segment joining
\theta
and
\theta_{0}
. Consequently, I want to show that

\displaystyle\sup_{(\theta,m)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})\times(\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n}))}\left|\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m)\right)\right|=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

(39)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{(\theta,m)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})\times(\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n}))}\left|\frac{1}{2}n(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\left(\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m)-\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})\right)(\theta-\theta_{0})\right|=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

(40)

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every sequence
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
.
For (
39
), Part 1 of the lemma and the fact that
M_{n}\rightarrow\infty
arbitrarily slowly as
n\rightarrow\infty
implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{(\theta,m)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})\times(\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n}))}\left|\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m)-\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq M_{n}\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\quad=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. For (
40
), I apply the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,
||Ax||_{2}\leq||A||_{op}||x||_{2}
for a matrix
A
and Euclidean vector
x
, and the triangle inequality to conclude that

\displaystyle\sup_{(\theta,m)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})\times(\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n}))}\left|\frac{1}{2}n(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\left(\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m)-\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})\right)(\theta-\theta_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq M_{n}^{2}\sup_{(\theta,m)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})\times(\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n}))}\left|\left|\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m)-\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{op}

\displaystyle\quad\leq M_{n}^{2}\sup_{(\theta,m)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})\times(\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n}))}\left|\left|\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m)-\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m_{0})\right|\right|_{op}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+M_{n}^{2}\sup_{\theta\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\left|\left|\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m_{0})-\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{op}

Since
\sup_{\theta\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},M_{n}/\sqrt{n})}||\tilde{\theta}_{n}||_{2}=O(1)
and the elements of
\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta,m)-\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta,m_{0})
are linear in
\theta
, Part 2 of the lemma implies the following convergence

\displaystyle\sup_{(\theta,m)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})\times(\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n}))}\left|\left|\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m)-\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m_{0})\right|\right|_{op}=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Moreover, since the mapping
\theta\mapsto\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta,m_{0})
is continuous over
B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},M_{n}/\sqrt{n})
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\left|\left|\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m_{0})-\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})\right|\right|_{op}=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Consequently, I conclude that if
M_{n}\rightarrow\infty
arbitrarily slowly as
n\rightarrow\infty
, then

\displaystyle\sup_{(\theta,m)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})\times(\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n}))}\left|\frac{1}{2}n(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\left(\tilde{I}_{n}(\tilde{\theta}_{n},m)-\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})\right)(\theta-\theta_{0})\right|\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma 5
.

Let
\{\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
be the sets defined in Assumptions
7
and
8
. If Assumptions
5
,
6
,
7
, and
8
hold, then for every
M_{n}\rightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\Pi\left((\beta,\xi)\in B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}((\beta_{0},\xi_{0}),M_{n}/\sqrt{n})^{c}|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},W_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n},m\right)\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where
B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}((\beta_{0},\xi_{0}),M_{n}/\sqrt{n})
is a Euclidean ball centered at
(\beta_{0},\xi_{0})
with radius
M_{n}/\sqrt{n}
.

Proof.

The proof has two steps. The first step derives a uniform upper bound for the numerator of the conditional posterior. The second step derives a uniform lower bound for the denominator of the conditional posterior that when combined with the first step implies the result. The argument is conceptually similar to the proof of Lemma
2
.

Step 1.
I start with a unifom upper bound for the numerator of the conditional posterior. Specifically, I show that there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\left((\beta_{0},\xi_{0}),\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{c}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)d\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\beta,\xi)\leq\exp(-CM_{n}^{2})\right)\rightarrow 1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Adding/subtracting
\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)
to obtain

\displaystyle\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)=\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)+\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)

reveals that it suffices to show that there exists constants
C_{1}>0
and
C_{2}>0
such that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\left((\beta_{0},\xi_{0}),\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{c}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)\right)d\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\beta,\xi)\leq\exp(-C_{1}M_{n}^{2})\right)\rightarrow 1

(41)

and

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\left((\beta_{0},\xi_{0}),\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{c}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)d\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\beta,\xi)\leq\exp(-C_{2}M_{n}^{2})\right)\rightarrow 1

(42)

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. For the bound (
41
), an expansion of the log-likelihood implies

\displaystyle\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\xi}{\xi_{0}}\left(\sqrt{n}(\beta-\beta_{0})^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{n}(\beta-\beta_{0})^{\prime}\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\sqrt{n}(\beta-\beta_{0})\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\xi}{\xi_{0}}\left(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi_{0},m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)

Consequently, I can apply a symmetric argument to that of Step 2 of Lemma
2
to conclude that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\sup_{\beta\in\mathcal{B}:||\beta-\beta_{0}||_{2}\geq\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi_{0},m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\underline{c}_{0}}{4}M_{n}^{2}\right)\right)\rightarrow 1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where
\underline{c}_{0}
is the constant defined in Part 3 of Lemma
4
. Since
\Xi=[\overline{\sigma}^{-2},\underline{\sigma}^{-2}]
, I able to further conclude that there exists a constant
C_{1}>0
such that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{(\beta,\xi)\in B((\beta_{0},\xi_{0}),M_{n}/\sqrt{n})^{c}}\exp(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m))\lesssim\exp(-C_{1}M_{n}^{2})\right)\rightarrow 1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. For (
42
), expanding the log-likelihood reveals that

\displaystyle\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)=\frac{n}{2}(\log\xi-\log\xi_{0})-\frac{n}{2}\left(\xi-\xi_{0}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(Y_{i}-m_{1}(w_{i})-(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta_{0}\right)^{2}

A second order Taylor expansion of
x\mapsto\log x
reveals that

\displaystyle\frac{n}{2}(\log\xi-\log\xi_{0})=\frac{n}{2\xi_{0}}(\xi-\xi_{0})-\frac{n}{2\tilde{\xi}^{2}_{n}}(\xi-\xi_{0})^{2},

where
\tilde{\xi}_{n}
on the line segment joining
\xi
and
\xi_{0}
. Since
\tilde{\xi}_{n}\geq\overline{\sigma}^{-2}
uniformly over
\Xi
, it follows that

\displaystyle\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{01},m)\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\xi_{0}}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}^{2}(m)\right)\sqrt{n}(\xi-\xi_{0})-\frac{1}{2\overline{\sigma}^{2}}(\sqrt{n}(\xi-\xi_{0}))^{2},

where
U_{i}(m)=Y_{i}-m_{1}(w_{i})-(X_{i}-m_{2}(w_{i}))^{\prime}\beta_{0}
for
i=1,...,n
. The proof of Part 1 of Lemma
4
shows that

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}^{2}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}^{2}(m)\right|\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Moreover, Chebyshev’s inequality reveals

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}^{2}-\frac{1}{\xi_{0}}=O_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Consequently,

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(U_{i}^{2}(m)-\frac{1}{\xi_{0}}\right)\right|\leq\frac{M_{n}}{2\overline{\sigma}^{2}}\right)\longrightarrow 1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Consequently, with
P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}
-probability approaching one, the following holds

\displaystyle\sup_{(\xi,m):|\xi-\xi_{0}|\geq\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}},m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)\leq\exp\left(\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}:t\geq M_{n}}\left\{\frac{M_{n}}{4\overline{\sigma}^{2}}t-\frac{1}{2\overline{\sigma}^{2}}t^{2}\right\}\right),

where
t=|\sqrt{n}(\xi-\xi_{0})|
. Solving the maximization problem to obtain
t=M_{n}
, I conclude that there is a constant
C_{2}>0
such that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\left((\beta_{0},\xi_{0}),\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{c}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)d\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\beta,\xi)\leq\exp(-C_{2}M_{n}^{2})\right)\rightarrow 1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Letting
C=C_{1}+C_{2}
, I conclude that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\left((\beta_{0},\xi_{0}),\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{c}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)d\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\beta,\xi)\leq\exp(-CM_{n}^{2})\right)\rightarrow 1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

Step 2.
Given the result of Step 1, it is sufficient to show that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\int_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)d\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\beta,\xi)\geq\exp\left(-\frac{C}{2}M_{n}^{2}\right)\right)\longrightarrow 1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Let
\theta=(\beta^{\prime},\xi)^{\prime}
, let
s=\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})
, and let
\mathcal{S}
be the image of
\mathcal{B}\times\Xi
under the mapping
\theta\mapsto s
. Applying the change of variables
\theta\mapsto s
, the posterior normalizing constant satisfies

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)d\Pi(\beta,\sigma_{1}^{2})

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{S}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}^{*}(s,m)-\ell_{n}^{*}(0,m)\right)d\Pi_{\mathcal{S}}(s)

\displaystyle\geq\int_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}^{*}(s,m)-\ell_{n}^{*}(0,m)\right)d\Pi_{\mathcal{S}}(s)

\displaystyle\geq K\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+1}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}^{*}(s,m)-\ell_{n}^{*}(0,m)\right)\pi_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}(s)ds,

where
\ell_{n}^{*}(s,m)=\ell_{n}(\theta_{0}+s/\sqrt{n},m)
,
\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}=\{s\in\mathcal{S}:||s||_{2}\leq\tilde{C}\}
for
\tilde{C}>0
,
\pi_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}(s)=\mathbf{1}\{s\in\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}\}/\lambda(\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}})
, and
K=\inf_{s\in\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}\pi_{\mathcal{S}}(s)/\lambda(\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}})
. Part 2 of Assumption
6
guarantees that
K\in(0,\infty)
. Consequently, the following inequality holds

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\int_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)d\Pi_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\beta,\xi)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{C}{2}M_{n}^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+1}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}^{*}(s,m)-\ell_{n}^{*}(0,m)\right)\pi_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}(s)ds\leq K^{-1}\exp\left(-\frac{C}{2}M_{n}^{2}\right)\right).

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
. Letting
s=(s_{1}^{\prime},s_{2})^{\prime}
with
s_{1}
corresponding to
\beta
and
s_{2}
corresponding to
\xi
, I know that

\displaystyle\ell_{n}^{*}(s,m)-\ell_{n}^{*}(0,m)=\left(\ell_{n}^{*}(s,m)-\ell_{n}^{*}((0,s_{2}),m)\right)+\left(\ell_{n}^{*}((0,s_{2}),m)-\ell_{n}^{*}(0,m)\right)

Taylor expansions of each term reveals that

\displaystyle\ell_{n}^{*}(s,m)-\ell_{n}^{*}((0,s_{2}),m)=s_{1}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}\left(\beta_{0},\xi_{0}+\frac{s_{2}}{\sqrt{n}},m\right)-\frac{1}{2}s_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{I}_{n,11}\left(\beta_{0},\xi_{0}+\frac{s_{2}}{\sqrt{n}},m\right)s_{1}

and

\displaystyle\ell_{n}^{*}((0^{\prime},s_{2})^{\prime},m)-\ell_{n}^{*}((0,s_{2})^{\prime},m)

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2}\left(\log\left(\xi_{0}+\frac{s_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\log\left(\xi_{0}\right)\right)-s_{2}\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}^{2}(m)

\displaystyle=s_{2}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\xi_{0}}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}^{2}(m)\right)-\frac{1}{2\xi_{0}^{2}}s_{2}^{2}+O\left(\frac{s_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

Using the definition of
\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta,\xi,m)
and
\tilde{I}_{n,11}(\beta,\xi,m)
, Lemma
4
, and that
||s||_{2}\leq\tilde{C}
, I know that the following holds:

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\left|s_{1}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}\left(\beta_{0},\xi_{0}+\frac{s_{2}}{\sqrt{n}},m\right)-s_{1}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, and, as a result,

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\inf_{s\in\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}\inf_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}s_{1}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}\left(\beta_{0},\xi_{0}+\frac{s_{2}}{\sqrt{n}},m\right)\geq-\frac{C}{16}M_{n}^{2}+s_{1}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right)\longrightarrow 1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Similarly,

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\left|\frac{\sqrt{n}}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\xi_{0}}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}^{2}(m)\right)-s_{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right|=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, and, as a result,

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\inf_{s\in\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}\inf_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}s_{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}\left(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m\right)\geq-\frac{C}{16}M_{n}^{2}+s_{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n,2}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right)\longrightarrow 1

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Moreover, Lemma
4
implies that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\sup_{s\in\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}\sup_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\frac{1}{2}s_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{I}_{n,11}\left(\beta_{0},\xi_{0}+\frac{s_{2}}{\sqrt{n}},m\right)s_{1}\leq\frac{C}{16}M_{n}^{2}\right)\longrightarrow 1

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, and, since
|s_{2}|\leq\tilde{C}
and
\xi_{01}^{2}\in(0,\infty)
,
\sup_{s:||s||_{2}\leq\tilde{C}}\frac{s_{2}^{2}}{2\xi_{0}^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{16}M_{n}^{2}
for large
n
. Consequently, I am able to conclude that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\inf_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B_{n,\mathcal{M}}(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+1}}\exp\left(\ell_{n}^{*}(s,m)-\ell_{n}^{*}(0,m)\right)\pi_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}(s)ds\leq K^{-1}\exp\left(-\frac{C}{2}M_{n}^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+1}}\exp\left(s^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right)\pi_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}(s)ds\leq K^{-1}\exp\left(-\frac{C}{4}M_{n}^{2}\right)\right\}\right)+o(1)

\displaystyle\leq P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+1}}s\pi_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}(s)ds\leq-\frac{C}{8}M_{n}^{2}\right)+o(1),

conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
, where the last inequality is an application of Jensen’s inequality and the fact that
-\log K\leq\frac{C}{8}M_{n}^{2}
for large
n
. Applying Chebyshev’s inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, I obtain the following inequalities

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+1}}s\pi_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}(s)ds\leq-\frac{C}{8}M_{n}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+1}}s\pi_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}(s)ds\right|\geq\frac{C}{8}M_{n}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{M_{n}^{4}}\left|\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+1}}s\pi_{\mathcal{S}_{\tilde{C}}}(s)ds\right|\right|_{2}^{2}E_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\sigma_{01}^{2},m_{0})\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=O(M_{n}^{-4}),

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
. Since
M_{n}\rightarrow\infty
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, I can then conclude that

\displaystyle P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}\left(\inf_{m\in\mathcal{M}_{n}\cap B(m_{0},D\delta_{n})}\int_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}\exp\left(\ell_{n}(\beta,\xi,m)-\ell_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m)\right)d\Pi(\beta,\sigma_{1}^{2})\leq\exp\left(-\frac{C}{2}M_{n}^{2}\right)\right)\longrightarrow 0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. This completes the proof.
∎

D.3
Extension of Theorem
1
to Multivariate
X
and Unknown
\sigma_{01}^{2}

The next result extends Theorem
1
to allow for multivariate
X
and unknown
\sigma_{01}^{2}
. The result is stated in terms of the joint posterior for
(\beta^{\prime},\xi)^{\prime}
, however, it implies the same result as Theorem
1
for the marginal posterior for
\beta
.

Theorem 3
.

Let
\theta=(\beta^{\prime},\xi)^{\prime}
and let
\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0}=\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})n^{-1/2}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})
. If Assumptions
5
,
6
,
7
, and
8
hold and
\theta_{0}\in\text{int}(\mathcal{B}\times\Xi)
, then

\displaystyle\left|\left|\Pi\left(\theta\in\cdot|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n}\right)-\mathcal{N}\left(\theta_{0}+\frac{\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0}}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{n}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\beta_{0},\xi_{0},m_{0})\right)\right|\right|_{TV}\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

Proof.

The argument is very similar to Theorem
1
except with appropriate modifications. Following symmetric arguments as Steps 1–3 of the proof of Theorem 1, Assumptions
5
–
8
lead me to deduce that

\displaystyle\Pi\left(\theta\in A\middle|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\int_{A\cap B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})^{\prime}\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})-\frac{n}{2}(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})(\theta-\theta_{0})\right)d\theta}{\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})^{\prime}\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})-\frac{n}{2}(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})(\theta-\theta_{0})\right)d\theta}+o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

for all events
A
, conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
. Recalling that
\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0}=n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\theta_{0},m_{0})\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})
, I complete the square to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\int_{A\cap B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})^{\prime}\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})-\frac{n}{2}(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})(\theta-\theta_{0})\right)d\theta}{\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\ell}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})^{\prime}\sqrt{n}(\theta-\theta_{0})-\frac{n}{2}(\theta-\theta_{0})^{\prime}\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})(\theta-\theta_{0})\right)d\theta}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\int_{A\cap B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}(\theta-\theta_{0}-n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0})^{\prime}n\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})(\theta-\theta_{0}-n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0})\right)d\theta}{\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}(\theta-\theta_{0}-n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0})^{\prime}n\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})(\theta-\theta_{0}-n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0})\right)d\theta}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\int_{A\cap B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\phi_{\theta_{0}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0},n^{-1}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\theta_{0},m_{0})}(\theta)d\theta}{\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\phi_{\theta_{0}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0},n^{-1}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\theta_{0},m_{0})}(\theta)d\theta},

where
\phi_{\theta_{0}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0},n^{-1}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\theta_{0},m_{0})}
is the probability density function of
\mathcal{N}(\theta_{0}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0},n^{-1}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\theta_{0}))
. This expression is analogous to that obtained in the final step of the proof of Theorem 1 (cf. equation (
A.1
)). Since
\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0}=O_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)
and
\tilde{I}_{n}(\theta_{0},m_{0})=O_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)
conditionally given
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
, it can be shown using analogous arguments to the proof of Theorem
1
that, if
M_{n}\rightarrow\infty
arbitrarily slowly, then

\displaystyle\sup_{A}\left|\frac{\int_{A\cap B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\phi_{\theta_{0}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0},n^{-1}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\theta_{0},m_{0})}(\theta)d\theta}{\int_{B_{\mathcal{B}\times\Xi}(\theta_{0},\frac{M_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})}\phi_{\theta_{0}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0},n^{-1}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\theta_{0},m_{0})}(\theta)d\theta}-\int_{A}\phi_{\theta_{0}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0},n^{-1}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\theta_{0},m_{0})}(\theta)d\theta\right|=o_{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}(1)

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. Consequently, an application of the triangle inequality allows me to conclude that

\displaystyle\sup_{A}\left|\Pi\left(\theta\in A\middle|\{(Y_{i},X_{i},w_{i}^{\prime})^{\prime}\}_{i=1}^{n}\right)-\int_{A}\phi_{\theta_{0}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\Delta}_{n,0},n^{-1}\tilde{I}_{n}^{-1}(\theta_{0},m_{0})}(\theta)d\theta\right|\overset{P_{0,YX|W}^{(n)}}{\longrightarrow}0

for
P_{0,W}^{\infty}
-almost every fixed realization
\{w_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
of
\{W_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. This completes the proof.
∎
